number[];\n28\t}\n29\t\n30\ttype StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | 'assistantIndex';\n31\ttype EntryFactory = typeof createThinkingEntry | typeof createAssistantEntry;\n32\texport function createMessageHandler(orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator) {\n33\t  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n34\t  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n35\t  return function handleMessage(\n36\t    ws: WebSocket,\n37\t    session: ChatSession,\n38\t    rawMessage: unknown,\n39\t    signal?: AbortSignal\n40\t  ): Promise<void> {\n41\t    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n42\t    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n43\t    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n44\t      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n45\t    }\n46\t    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n47\t    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, validation));\n48\t  };\n49\t}\n50\t\n51\t// The whole answer, an error and the stream_end included, goes out inside the lock: an error frame names no message.\n52\tasync function answerInTurn(\n53\t  ws: WebSocket,\n54\t  session: ChatSession,\n55\t  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n56\t  validation: ValidationResult,\n57\t  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n58\t): Promise<void> {\n59\t  // Its socket closed while it waited: nobody is left to read the answer, and a session's last close took its credentials.\n60\t  if (signal?.aborted || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSING || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSED) {\n61\t    log.info('message', 'queued message dropped: its socket closed', {sessionId: session.sessionId});\n62\t    return;\n63\t  }\n64\t  const startedAt = Date.now();\n65\t  try {\n66\t    await wsErrorHandler(ws, () => answerUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n67\t  } finally {\n68\t    endTurn(ws, session, signal, startedAt);\n69\t  }\n70\t}\n71\t\n72\t// A turn whose socket closed is abandoned rather than ended: nobody is left to read a terminal frame.\n73\tfunction endTurn(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, signal: AbortSignal | undefined, startedAt: number): void {\n74\t  if (signal?.aborted) {\n75\t    log.info('message', 'turn abandoned: its socket closed', {\n76\t      sessionId: session.sessionId,\n77\t      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n78\t    });\n79\t    return;\n80\t  }\n81\t  sendStreamEnd(ws);\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\tasync function answerUserMessage(\n85\t  ws: WebSocket,\n86\t  session: ChatSession,\n87\t  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n88\t  validation: ValidationResult,\n89\t  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n90\t): Promise<void> {\n91\t  const message = validation.valid ? validation.data : undefined;\n92\t  if (message?.type === 'user_message') return handleUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, message.content, signal);\n93\t  answerFrame(ws, validation);\n94\t}\n95\t\n96\tfunction answerFrame(ws: WebSocket, validation: ValidationResult): void {\n97\t  if (!validation.valid) {\n98\t    log.warn('message', 'validation failed', {error: validation.error});\n99\t    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_MESSAGE, validation.error);\n100\t    return;\n101\t  }\n102\t\n103\t  const message = validation.data;\n104\t  if (message.type === 'ping') handlePing(ws, message.timestamp);\n105\t}\n106\t\n107\t// Read from the raw frame, so a user_message that fails validation still gets its stream_end.\n108\tfunction isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage: unknown): boolean {\n109\t  return typeof rawMessage === 'object' && rawMessage !== null && Reflect.get(rawMessage, 'type') === 'user_message';\n110\t}\n111\t\n112\tasync function wsErrorHandler(ws: WebSocket, action: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n113\t  try {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport {ErrorCodes} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport {ThinkingTagParser, ParsedSegment} from '../../infrastructure/parsers/thinkingTagParser.js';\nimport {validateMessage} from './validation.js';\nimport type {ValidationResult} from './validation.js';\nimport {PerSessionLock} from '../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport type {MessageChunk} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport {\n  createUserEntry,\n  createAssistantEntry,\n  createThinkingEntry,\n  createToolEntry,\n} from '../../orchestration/entryFactories.js';\nimport {appendEntry, appendDelta, commitEntry, markTruncated} from '../../orchestration/entryMutations.js';\nimport {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd} from './sender.js';\nimport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n\ninterface StreamState {\n  thinkingIndex: number | null;\n  assistantIndex: number | null;\n  suppressOutput: boolean;\n  sentAssistantIndices: number[];\n}\n\ntype StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | 'assistantIndex';\ntype EntryFactory = typeof createThinkingEntry | typeof createAssistantEntry;\nexport function createMessageHandler(orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator) {\n  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n  return function handleMessage(\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n    }\n    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, validation));\n  };\n}\n\n// The whole answer, an error and the stream_end included, goes out inside the lock: an error frame names no message.\nasync function answerInTurn(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  // Its socket closed while it waited: nobody is left to read the answer, and a session's last close took its credentials.\n  if (signal?.aborted || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSING || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSED) {\n    log.info('message', 'queued message dropped: its socket closed', {sessionId: session.sessionId});\n    return;\n  }\n  const startedAt = Date.now();\n  try {\n    await wsErrorHandler(ws, () => answerUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n  } finally {\n    endTurn(ws, session, signal, startedAt);\n  }\n}\n\n// A turn whose socket closed is abandoned rather than ended: nobody is left to read a terminal frame.\nfunction endTurn(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, signal: AbortSignal | undefined, startedAt: number): void {\n  if (signal?.aborted) {\n    log.info('message', 'turn abandoned: its socket closed', {\n      sessionId: session.sessionId,\n      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n  sendStreamEnd(ws);\n}\n\nasync function answerUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  const message = validation.valid ? validation.data : undefined;\n  if (message?.type === 'user_message') return handleUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, message.content, signal);\n  answerFrame(ws, validation);\n}\n\nfunction answerFrame(ws: WebSocket, validation: ValidationResult): void {\n  if (!validation.valid) {\n    log.warn('message', 'validation failed', {error: validation.error});\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_MESSAGE, validation.error);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const message = validation.data;\n  if (message.type === 'ping') handlePing(ws, message.timestamp);\n}\n\n// Read from the raw frame, so a user_message that fails validation still gets its stream_end.\nfunction isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage: unknown): boolean {\n  return typeof rawMessage === 'object' && rawMessage !== null && Reflect.get(rawMessage, 'type') === 'user_message';\n}\n\nasync function wsErrorHandler(ws: WebSocket, action: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await action();\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';\n    const code = error instanceof AIError ? error.code : 'UNKNOWN';\n    log.error('message', 'handling error', {error: message, code});\n    if (error instanceof AIError && error.code === 'RATE_LIMITED') {\n      sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.RATE_LIMITED, 'AI service is busy, please try again');\n      return;\n    }\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.SERVER_ERROR, 'Failed to process message');\n  }\n}\n\nasync function handleUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  content: string,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  log.info('message', 'user message received', {sessionId: session.sessionId, contentLength: content.length});\n\n  const userEntry = createUserEntry(content);\n  const userIndex = appendEntry(session, userEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, userIndex, userEntry);\n\n  const parser = new ThinkingTagParser();\n  const state: StreamState = {\n    thinkingIndex: null,\n    assistantIndex: null,\n    suppressOutput: false,\n    sentAssistantIndices: [],\n  };\n\n  let textChunkCount = 0;\n\n  try {\n    for await (const chunk of orchestrator.processMessageStream(session, {signal})) {\n      // Its socket closed: breaking returns the run's generator, so the run stops and its own teardown still runs.\n      if (signal?.aborted) break;\n      textChunkCount = logChunk(chunk, textChunkCount);\n      processChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk);\n    }\n  } finally {\n    // Anything still open here was cut short: a stop has already flushed the parser and committed every entry it opened.\n    flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n  }\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n  log.info('stream', 'stream completed', {suppressed: state.suppressOutput, entries: session.entries.length});\n}\n\n// Diagnostics only: counts a run of text chunks so it is logged once, not per token.\nfunction logChunk(chunk: MessageChunk, textChunkCount: number): number {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return textChunkCount + 1;\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') {\n    log.debug('stream', 'tool_result chunk', {\n      messageCount: chunk.clientMessages.length,\n      types: chunk.clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n    });\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  log.debug('stream', 'chunk', {type: chunk.type});\n  return 0;\n}\n\nfunction processChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  chunk: MessageChunk\n): void {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return processTextChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk.text);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_use') return processToolUseChunk(ws, session, chunk.toolCall);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') return processToolResultChunk(ws, chunk.clientMessages);\n  if (chunk.type === 'suppress_output') return processSuppressChunk(ws, session, state);\n  if (chunk.type === 'stop') return processStopChunk(ws, session, state, parser);\n}\n\nfunction processTextChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  text: string\n): void {\n  const segments = parser.push(text);\n  for (const segment of segments) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction handleTextSegment(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, segment: ParsedSegment): void {\n  if (segment.type === 'thinking') {\n    streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex', createThinkingEntry, segment.content);\n    return;\n  }\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex', createAssistantEntry, segment.content);\n}\n\nfunction streamSegment(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  key: StateIndexKey,\n  createEntry: EntryFactory,\n  content: string\n): void {\n  const isNew = initSegment(session, state, key, createEntry);\n  if (isNew && state.suppressOutput) skipSuppressed(ws, session, state[key]!);\n  if (isNew && !state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, state[key]!, session.entries[state[key]!]);\n  appendDelta(session, state[key]!, content);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendDelta(ws, state[key]!, content);\n}\n\n// Stored as suppressed, so a replay on resume shows the client what it saw live and not the text a tool hid.\nfunction skipSuppressed(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  markSuppressed(session, index);\n  sendSkip(ws, index);\n}\n\nfunction markSuppressed(session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  const entry = session.entries[index];\n  if (entry.role === 'assistant' || entry.role === 'thinking') entry.suppressed = true;\n}\n\nfunction initSegment(session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey, createEntry: EntryFactory): boolean {\n  if (state[key] !== null) return false;\n  state[key] = appendEntry(session, createEntry());\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction commitAndClear(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n\n  commitEntry(session, index);\n  state[key] = null;\n\n  // Only what the client received can be retracted, and only assistant entries.\n  if (state.suppressOutput) return;\n\n  sendCommit(ws, index);\n  if (key === 'assistantIndex') state.sentAssistantIndices.push(index);\n}\n\nfunction processToolUseChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  toolCall: {name: string; input: Record<string, unknown>}\n): void {\n  const toolEntry = createToolEntry(toolCall.name, toolCall.input);\n  const toolIndex = appendEntry(session, toolEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, toolIndex, toolEntry);\n}\n\nfunction processToolResultChunk(ws: WebSocket, clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[]): void {\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    log.debug('stream', 'forwarding client message to frontend', {\n      type: msg.type,\n      index: 'index' in msg ? msg.index : undefined,\n    });\n    sendJson(ws, msg);\n  }\n}\n\n// Retracts what the client saw: every assistant entry sent so far is re-sent blank.\nfunction processSuppressChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  // Closed as streamed so far: an open answer is then retracted like a sent one, and what follows opens new entries.\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n  log.debug('stream', 'suppression activated', {blankingEntries: state.sentAssistantIndices});\n\n  for (const idx of state.sentAssistantIndices) {\n    sendUpsert(ws, idx, {...session.entries[idx], content: ''} as SessionEntry);\n    markSuppressed(session, idx);\n  }\n\n  state.sentAssistantIndices = [];\n  state.suppressOutput = true;\n}\n\nfunction processStopChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n}\n\n// The parser holds back a tail that might open a tag; without this the tail of a cut-off answer would be lost.\nfunction flushHeldText(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  for (const segment of parser.flush()) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\n// The flag lives on the stored entry even when suppressed; only a visible entry is re-sent, which is how a live client learns it.\nfunction closeCutOff(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n  markTruncated(session, index);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, index, session.entries[index]);\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, key);\n}\n\nfunction commitOpenEntries(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n}\n\nfunction handlePing(ws: WebSocket, timestamp: number): void {\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'pong', timestamp}));\n}\n","numLines":336,"startLine":1,"totalLines":336}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_f53f7427ec0c40cfbe61a4a9","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{\n11\t  valid: false;\n12\t  error: string;\n13\t}\n14\t\n15\texport type ValidationResult = ValidationSuccess | ValidationFailure;\n16\t\n17\texport function validateMessage(payload: unknown): {\n  valid: false;\n  error: string;\n}\n\nexport type ValidationResult = ValidationSuccess | ValidationFailure;\n\nexport function validateMessage(payload: unknown): ValidationResult {\n  if (typeof payload !== 'object' || payload === null) {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'Message must be an object'};\n  }\n\n  const message = payload as Record<string, unknown>;\n\n  if (typeof message.type {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_a958eb6bfc1246ae9519c437","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport http from 'http';\n2\timport {WebSocketServer} from 'ws';\n3\timport type {Express} from 'express';\n4\timport type {WsAuthenticator} from '../types/auth.js';\n5\timport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\n6\timport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\n7\timport type {ToolRegistry} from '../orchestration/tools/registry.js';\n8\timport {createUpgradeHandler, createConnectionHandler, ExtWebSocket} from './ws/connectionHandler.js';\n9\timport {createMessageHandler} from './ws/messageHandler.js';\n10\timport {selectSubprotocol} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\n11\timport type {ConnectInfo, SessionResumeOptions} from './ws/sessionResume.js';\n12\timport {log} from '../shared/logger.js';\n13\t\n14\texport interface HalServerOptions {\n15\t  app: Express;\n16\t  wsAuth: WsAuthenticator;\n17\t  sessionStore: SessionStore;\n18\t  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator;\n19\t  toolRegistry?: ToolRegistry;\n20\t  basePath?: string;\n21\t  port?: number;\n22\t  heartbeatIntervalMs?: number;\n23\t  onConnect?: (session: import('../types/session.js').ChatSession, connection: ConnectInfo) => void | Promise<void>;\n24\t  onDisconnect?: (sessionId: string) => void | Promise<void>;\n25\t  resume?: SessionResumeOptions;\n26\t}\n27\t\n28\texport interface HalServer {\n29\t  server: http.Server;\n30\t  wss: WebSocketServer;\n31\t  start: (port?: number) => Promise<void>;\n32\t  stop: () => Promise<void>;\n33\t}\n34\t\n35\texport function createServer(options: HalServerOptions): HalServer {\n36\t  const basePath = options.basePath ?? '/hal';\n37\t\n38\t  const wss = new WebSocketServer({\n39\t    noServer: true,\n40\t    handleProtocols: selectSubprotocol,\n41\t  });\n42\t\n43\t  const handleMessage = createMessageHandler(options.orchestrator);\n44\t  const examplePrompts = options.toolRegistry?.getExamplePrompts() ?? [];\n45\t\n46\t  const deps = {\n47\t    wsAuth: options.wsAuth,\n48\t    sessionStore: options.sessionStore,\n49\t    handleMessage,\n50\t    basePath,\n51\t    onConnect: options.onConnect,\n52\t    onDisconnect: options.onDisconnect,\n53\t    resume: options.resume,\n54\t  };\n55\t\n56\t  const handleUpgrade = createUpgradeHandler(wss, deps);\n57\t  const handleConnection = createConnectionHandler(deps, examplePrompts);\n58\t\n59\t  const server = http.createServer(options.app);\n60\t\n61\t  server.on('upgrade', (request, socket, head) => {\n62\t    handleUpgrade(request, socket, head);\n63\t  });\n64\t\n65\t  wss.on('connection', ws => {\n66\t    handleConnection(ws as ExtWebSocket);\n67\t  });\n68\t\n69\t  const heartbeatMs = options.heartbeatIntervalMs ?? 30_000;\n70\t\n71\t  const heartbeatInterval = setInterval(() => {\n72\t    wss.clients.forEach(ws => {\n73\t      const extWs = ws as ExtWebSocket;\n74\t      if (!extWs.isAlive) {\n75\t        extWs.terminate();\n76\t        return;\n77\t      }\n78\t      extWs.isAlive = false;\n79\t      extWs.ping();\n80\t    });\n81\t  }, heartbeatMs);\n82\t\n83\t  // The listening socket should hold the process open, not the heartbeat: an engine never started must not.\n84\t  heartbeatInterval.unref();\n85\t\n86\t  wss.on('close', () => clearInterval(heartbeatInterval));\n87\t\n88\t  return {\n89\t    server,\n90\t    wss,\n91\t    start: (port?: number) =>\n92\t      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n93\t        // Refused before the handlers are touched: `listen` throws on a running server, and the swap below stripped its handler.\n94\t        if (server.listening) {\n95\t          reject(new Error('HAL Engine is already started'));\n96\t          return;\n97\t        }\n98\t\n99\t        // ?? not ||, so a configured port 0 means \"let the OS choose\" rather than 8086.\n100\t        const p = port ?? options.port ?? (Number(process.env.PORT) || 8086);\n101\t\n102\t        // Without this an EADDRINUSE settles nothing and takes the process down as an unhandled event.\n103\t        const onError = (error: Error) => reject(error);\n104\t        server.once('error', onError);\n105\t\n106\t        // A listener left from a previous start would report this bind failure as a running server breaking.\n107\t        server.removeListener('error', logLateError);\n108\t\n109\t        server.listen(p, () => {\n110\t          server.removeListener('error', onError);\n111\t\n112\t          // Past the listen window nothing is listening, and an 'error' with no handler ends the process.\n113\t          server.on('error', logLateError);\n114\t\n115\t          // The bound port, not the requested one: a configured 0 means the OS chose it.\n116\t          log.info('server', 'HAL Engine started', {port: boundPort(server, p)});\n117\t          resolve();\n118\t        });\n119\t      }),\n120\t    stop: () =>\n121\t      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n122\t        wss.clients.forEach(ws => ws.close(1001, 'Server shutting down'));\n123\t        wss.close();\n124\t        clearInterval(heartbeatInterval);\n125\t\n126\t        // Nothing to close after a refused start or a second stop: `close` would reject for a server that never ran.\n127\t        if (!server.listening) {\n128\t          resolve();\n129\t          return;\n130\t        }\n131\t        server.close(err => (err ? reject(err) : resolve()));\n132\t      }),\n133\t  };\n134\t}\n135\t\n136\t// A running server's error is a condition to report, not a reason to exit; the consumer decides what to do.\n137\tfunction logLateError(error: Error): void {\n138\t  log.error('server', 'server error after start', {error: error.message});\n139\t}\n140\t\n141\tfunction boundPort(server: http.Server, requested: number): number {\n142\t  const address = server.address();\n143\t  return typeof address === 'object' && address !== null ? address.port : requested;\n144\t}\n145\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f8c91476-3213-4a4d-9278-f34d2b15649c","uuid":"54059159-a4ad-4b1d-820f-dbb78a2c7a39","timestamp":"2026-11-02T23:56:20.037Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/97acaca6da89/workspace/target/src/transport/createServer.ts","content":"import http from 'http';\nimport {WebSocketServer} from 'ws';\nimport type {Express} from 'express';\nimport type {WsAuthenticator} from '../types/auth.js';\nimport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {ToolRegistry} from '../orchestration/tools/registry.js';\nimport {createUpgradeHandler, createConnectionHandler, ExtWebSocket} from './ws/connectionHandler.js';\nimport {createMessageHandler} from './ws/messageHandler.js';\nimport {selectSubprotocol} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\nimport type {ConnectInfo, SessionResumeOptions} from './ws/sessionResume.js';\nimport {log} from '../shared/logger.js';\n\nexport interface HalServerOptions {\n  app: Express;\n  wsAuth: WsAuthenticator;\n  sessionStore: SessionStore;\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator;\n  toolRegistry?: ToolRegistry;\n  basePath?: string;\n  port?: number;\n  heartbeatIntervalMs?: number;\n  onConnect?: (session: import('../types/session.js').ChatSession, connection: ConnectInfo) => void | Promise<void>;\n  onDisconnect?: (sessionId: string) => void | Promise<void>;\n  resume?: SessionResumeOptions;\n}\n\nexport interface HalServer {\n  server: http.Server;\n  wss: WebSocketServer;\n  start: (port?: number) => Promise<void>;\n  stop: () => Promise<void>;\n}\n\nexport function createServer(options: HalServerOptions): HalServer {\n  const basePath = options.basePath ?? '/hal';\n\n  const wss = new WebSocketServer({\n    noServer: true,\n    handleProtocols: selectSubprotocol,\n  });\n\n  const handleMessage = createMessageHandler(options.orchestrator);\n  const examplePrompts = options.toolRegistry?.getExamplePrompts() ?? [];\n\n  const deps = {\n    wsAuth: options.wsAuth,\n    sessionStore: options.sessionStore,\n    handleMessage,\n    basePath,\n    onConnect: options.onConnect,\n    onDisconnect: options.onDisconnect,\n    resume: options.resume,\n  };\n\n  const handleUpgrade = createUpgradeHandler(wss, deps);\n  const handleConnection = createConnectionHandler(deps, examplePrompts);\n\n  const server = http.createServer(options.app);\n\n  server.on('upgrade', (request, socket, head) => {\n    handleUpgrade(request, socket, head);\n  });\n\n  wss.on('connection', ws => {\n    handleConnection(ws as ExtWebSocket);\n  });\n\n  const heartbeatMs = options.heartbeatIntervalMs ?? 30_000;\n\n  const heartbeatInterval = setInterval(() => {\n    wss.clients.forEach(ws => {\n      const extWs = ws as ExtWebSocket;\n      if (!extWs.isAlive) {\n        extWs.terminate();\n        return;\n      }\n      extWs.isAlive = false;\n      extWs.ping();\n    });\n  }, heartbeatMs);\n\n  // The listening socket should hold the process open, not the heartbeat: an engine never started must not.\n  heartbeatInterval.unref();\n\n  wss.on('close', () => clearInterval(heartbeatInterval));\n\n  return {\n    server,\n    wss,\n    start: (port?: number) =>\n      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n        // Refused before the handlers are touched: `listen` throws on a running server, and the swap below stripped its handler.\n        if (server.listening) {\n          reject(new Error('HAL Engine is already started'));\n          return;\n        }\n\n        // ?? not ||, so a configured port 0 means \"let the OS choose\" rather than 8086.\n        const p = port ?? options.port ?? (Number(process.env.PORT) || 8086);\n\n        // Without this an EADDRINUSE settles nothing and takes the process down as an unhandled event.\n        const onError = (error: Error) => reject(error);\n        server.once('error', onError);\n\n        // A listener left from a previous start would report this bind failure as a running server breaking.\n        server.removeListener('error', logLateError);\n\n        server.listen(p, () => {\n          server.removeListener('error', onError);\n\n          // Past the listen window nothing is listening, and an 'error' with no handler ends the process.\n          server.on('error', logLateError);\n\n          // The bound port, not the requested one: a configured 0 means the OS chose it.\n          log.info('server', 'HAL Engine started', {port: boundPort(server, p)});\n          resolve();\n        });\n      }),\n    stop: () =>\n      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n        wss.clients.forEach(ws => ws.close(1001, 'Server shutting down'));\n        wss.close();\n        clearInterval(heartbeatInterval);\n\n        // Nothing to close after a refused start or a second stop: `close` would reject for a server that never ran.\n        if (!server.listening) {\n          resolve();\n          return;\n        }\n        server.close(err => (err ? reject(err) : resolve()));\n      }),\n  };\n}\n\n// A running server's error is a condition to report, not a reason to exit; the consumer decides what to do.\nfunction logLateError(error: Error): void {\n  log.error('server', 'server error after start', {error: error.message});\n}\n\nfunction boundPort(server: http.Server, requested: number): number {\n  const address = server.address();\n  return typeof address === 'object' && address !== null ? address.port : requested;\n}\n","numLines":145,"startLine":1,"totalLines":145}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_a958eb6bfc1246ae9519c437","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

SessionResumeOptions} from './sessionResume.js';\n15\timport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n16\t\n17\texport interface ExtWebSocket extends WebSocket {\n18\t  isAlive: boolean;\n19\t  userId: string;\n27\t  startNewSession?: boolean;\n28\t}\n29\t\n30\texport interface ConnectionHandlerDeps {\n31\t  wsAuth: WsAuthenticator;\n32\t  sessionStore: SessionStore;\n33\t  /** `signal` is the connection's own, aborted when its socket closes (specs/hal-engine-abandon-on-close/spec.md). */\n34\t  handleMessage: (\n35\t    ws: WebSocket,\n36\t    session: import('../../types/session.js').ChatSession,\n37\t    rawMessage: unknown,\n38\t    signal?: AbortSignal\n39\t  ) => Promise<void>;\n40\t  basePath: string;\n41\t  onConnect?: (session: import('../../types/session.js').ChatSession, connection: ConnectInfo) => void | Promise<void>;\n42\t  onDisconnect?: (sessionId: string) => void | Promise<void>;\n43\t  resume?: SessionResumeOptions;\n44\t}\n45\texport function createUpgradeHandler(wss: WebSocketServer, deps: ConnectionHandlerDeps) {\n46\t  return function handleUpgrade(request: IncomingMessage, socket: Duplex, head: Buffer): void {\n47\t    // Nothing may throw out of this listener: the `upgrade` event has no catch above it, so a throw ends the process.\n48\t    if (!request.headers.host || !isValidWsPath(request.url || '', deps.basePath)) {\n49\t      rejectSocket(socket, '400 Bad Request');\n50\t      return;\n51\t    }\n52\t\n53\t    authenticate(deps.wsAuth, request)\n54\t      .then(user => {\n55\t        if (!user) {\n56\t          rejectSocket(socket, '401 Unauthorized');\n57\t          return;\n58\t        }\n59\t\n60\t        wss.handleUpgrade(request, socket, head, ws => {\n61\t          const extWs = ws as ExtWebSocket;\n62\t          extWs.userId = user.id;\n63\t          extWs.workspaceId = user['workspaceId'] as string | number | undefined;\n64\t          extWs.authHeaders = {\n65\t            cookie: request.headers.cookie,\n66\t            authorization: request.headers.authorization || bearerFromWebSocketProtocol(request),\n67\t            host: (request.headers['x-forwarded-host'] as string | undefined) ?? request.headers.host,\n68\t          };\n69\t          extWs.isAlive = true;\n70\t          if (deps.resume?.enabled) {\n71\t {ConnectInfo, SessionResumeOptions} from './sessionResume.js';\nimport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n\nexport interface ExtWebSocket extends WebSocket {\n  isAlive: boolean;\n  userId: string (specs/hal-engine-abandon-on-close/spec.md). */\n  handleMessage: (\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: import('../../types/session.js').ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ) => Promise<void>;\n  basePath: string;\n  onConnect?: (session: import('../../types/session.js').ChatSession, connection: ConnectInfo) => void | Promise<void>;\n  onDisconnect?: (sessionId: string) => void | Promise<void>;\n  resume?: SessionResumeOptions;\n}\nexport function createUpgradeHandler(wss: WebSocketServer, deps: ConnectionHandlerDeps) {\n  return function handleUpgrade(request: IncomingMessage, socket: Duplex, head: Buffer): void {\n    // Nothing may throw out of this listener: the `upgrade` event has no catch above it, so a throw ends the process.\n    if (!request.headers.host || !isValidWsPath(request.url || '', deps.basePath)) {\n      rejectSocket(socket, '400 Bad Request');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    authenticate(deps.wsAuth, request)\n      .then(user => {\n        if (!user) {\n          rejectSocket(socket, '401 Unauthorized');\n          return;\n        }\n\n        wss.handleUpgrade(request, socket, head, ws => {\n          const extWs = ws as ExtWebSocket;\n          extWs.userId = user.id;\n          extWs.workspaceId = user['workspaceId'] as string | number | undefined;\n          extWs.authHeaders = {\n            cookie: request.headers.cookie,\n            authorization: request.headers.authorization || bearerFromWebSocketProtocol(request),\n            host: (request.headers['x-forwarded-host'] as string | undefined) ?? request.headers.host,\n          };\n          extWs.isAlive = true;\n          if (deps.resume?.enabled) {\n            extWs.requestedSessionId = sessionIdFromUpgrade(request.url || '', deps.basePath);\n            extWs.startNewSession = newSessionRequested(request.url || '', deps.basePath);\n          }\n          wss.emit('connection', extWs, request);\n        });\n      })\n      .catch((error: unknown) => {\n        // The type alone: an authenticator's message can carry the credential it was checking.\n        log.error('ws', 'authenticator failed', {errorType: error instanceof Error ? error.name : typeof error});\n        rejectSocket(socket, '500 Internal Server Error');\n      });\n  };\n}\n\n// Async, so a synchronous throw becomes a rejection (answered 500) and a user returned without a promise still resolves.\nasync function authenticate(wsAuth: WsAuthenticator, request: IncomingMessage): Promise<AuthenticatedUser | null> {\n  return wsAuth(request);\n}\n\nfunction bearerFromWebSocketProtocol(req: IncomingMessage): string | undefined {\n  const token = credentialFromSubprotocol(req.headers['sec-websocket-protocol']);\n  return token ? `Bearer ${token}` : undefined;\n}\n\nexport function createConnectionHandler(deps: ConnectionHandlerDeps, examplePrompts: string[]) {\n  return function handleConnection(ws: ExtWebSocket): void {\n    // The listener stays synchronous: an async one rejects into the emitter, and node ends the process on that.\n    openSession(deps, examplePrompts, ws).catch((error: unknown) => {\n ws.close(1011, 'Connection setup failed');\n    });\n  };\n}\n\n// The sockets open on each session, oldest first: a resumed session can be open on more than one at once.\nconst openSockets = new ws);\n  held.session = session;\n\n  log.info('ws', 'connected', {sessionId, userId: ws.userId});\n\n  const connected: ConnectedMessage = {\n    type: 'connected',\n    sessionId,\n    message: 'Connected to HAL Engine',\n    examplePrompts,\n    ...resumeFields(deps.resume, outcome),\n  };\n  // A socket that died between the check above and this frame must not leave the session it was error: messageOf(error)});\n    await evictUnlessResumed(deps, sessionId, resumed);\n    endSession(deps, sessionId, state, held);\n    ws.resume();\n    ws.close(1011, 'Connection setup failed');\n drains before the loop delivers any inbound frame on this socket.\n  queueMicrotask(() =>\n    runHook('onConnect', sessionId, () => deps.onConnect?.(session, {resumed: resumed !== undefined}))\n  );\n\n  ws.on('message', (frame: Buffer | string) => {\n    const parsed = parseWsData(frame);\n    if (parsed === null) {\n      sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_FORMAT, 'Invalid JSON');\n      return;\n    }\n    log.info('ws', 'message received', {sessionId, type: (parsed as Record<string, unknown>).type as string});\n    deps.handleMessage(ws, session, parsed, connection.signal);\n  });\n\n  ws.resume();\n}\n\n// Joined as the newest socket on the session, which carries this upgrade's credentials from here on.\nfunction join(session: ChatSession, ws: ExtWebSocket): void {\n  openSockets.set(session, [...(openSockets.get(session) ?? []), ws]);\n  session.authHeaders = ws.authHeaders;\n}\n\n// `undefined` rather than a throw: one store failure closes one socket instead of ending the process.\nasync function createSession(\n  deps: ConnectionHandlerDeps,\n  sessionId: string,\n  ws: ExtWebSocket\n): Promise<ChatSession | undefined> {\n  try {\n    return await deps.sessionStore.create(sessionId, ws.userId, {\n      authHeaders: ws.authHeaders,\n      workspaceId: ws.workspaceId,\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    log.error('ws', 'session create failed', {userId: ws.userId, error: messageOf(error)});\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.SERVER_ERROR, 'Could not start a session');\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\n// The close path erases nothing: the conversation outlives its socket, and `onDisconnect` is the consumer's seam.\nfunction endSession(\n  deps: ConnectionHandlerDeps,\n  sessionId: string,\n  state: {notified: boolean; delivered: boolean},\n  held: {session?: ChatSession; socket: ExtWebSocket}\n): void {\n  if (state.notified) return;\n  state.notified = true;\n\n  if (held.session) leave(held.session, held.socket);\n\n  // Only for a session the consumer was actually handed: onDisconnect is the other half of onConnect, not of a socket.\n  if (!state.delivered) return;\n\n  runHook('onDisconnect', sessionId, () => deps.onDisconnect?.(sessionId));\n}\n\n// The newest socket still open lends the session its credentials; with none left, nothing keeps them or a closed socket.\nfunction leave(session: ChatSession, ws: ExtWebSocket): void {\n  const open = openSockets.get(session) ?? session holds the conversation being rejoined.\nasync function evictUnlessResumed(\n  deps: ConnectionHandlerDeps,\n  sessionId: string,\n  resumed: ChatSession | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  if {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_a8909ef5337c411582073ca7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport type {AddressInfo} from 'node:net';\n2\timport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\n3\timport type {Express} from 'express';\n4\timport {createServer} from './createServer.js';\n5\timport type {HalServer, HalServerOptions} from './createServer.js';\n6\timport {HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\n7\timport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\n8\timport type {MessageChunk} from '../types/ai.js';\n9\timport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\n10\timport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\n11\t\n12\t// Shared by the suites that need a real upgrade over a real socket, which no double can stand in for.\n13\t\n14\tconst servers: Array<{stop: () => Promise<void>}> = [];\n15\tconst clients: WebSocket[] = [];\n16\t\n17\t/** An orchestrator that answers once and stops, which is what commits the turn's assistant entry. */\n18\texport const answeringOrchestrator: ChatOrchestrator = {\n19\t  processMessage: async () => 'hi',\n20\t  async *processMessageStream(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n21\t    yield {type: 'text', text: 'hi'};\n22\t    yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n23\t  },\n24\t};\n25\t\n26\t/** Starts an engine on an ephemeral port and returns the URL a client should open. */\n27\texport async function startEngine(\n28\t  sessionStore: SessionStore,\n29\t  onDisconnect?: (sessionId: string) => void\n30\t): Promise<string> {\n31\t  const {url} = await startEngineWith({sessionStore, onDisconnect});\n32\t  return url;\n33\t}\n34\t\n35\t/** Starts an engine with whatever a suite overrides, returning the server itself beside the URL a client should open. */\n36\texport async function startEngineWith(\n37\t  options: Partial<HalServerOptions> & Pick<HalServerOptions, 'sessionStore'>\n38\t): Promise<{url: string; hal: HalServer}> {\n39\t  const hal = createServer({\n40\t    app: (() => undefined) as unknown as Express,\n41\t    wsAuth: async () => ({id: 'u1'}),\n42\t    orchestrator: answeringOrchestrator,\n43\t    ...options,\n44\t  });\n45\t  await hal.start(0);\n46\t  servers.push(hal);\n47\t  const {port} = hal.server.address() as AddressInfo;\n48\t  return {url: `ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/hal/ws/c1`, hal};\n49\t}\n50\t\n51\t/** Opens a client that the suite's teardown will close, whether its test passed or failed. */\n52\texport function connectClient(url: string, token = 't'): WebSocket {\n53\t  const client = new WebSocket(url, [HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL, token]);\n54\t  clients.push(client);\n55\t  return client;\n56\t}\n57\t\n58\t/** A promise a test settles by hand, to hold a stream or a hook at a point of its choosing. */\n59\texport function deferred(): {until: Promise<void>; release: () => void} {\n60\t  let release = (): void => undefined;\n61\t  const until = new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n62\t    release = resolve;\n63\t  });\n64\t  return {until, release};\n65\t}\n66\t\n67\t/** Resolves with every frame type seen up to and including the first `until` frame. */\n68\texport function framesUntil(client: WebSocket, until: string): Promise<string[]> {\n69\t  const seen: string[] = [];\n70\t  return new Promise<string[]>(resolve => {\n71\t    client.on('message', raw => {\n72\t      const {type} = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as {type: string};\n73\t      seen.push(type);\n74\t      if (type === until) resolve(seen);\n75\t    });\n76\t  });\n77\t}\n78\t\n79\t/** Opens a client that asks one question the moment the socket opens, resolving on the first `until` frame. */\n80\texport function askOnOpen(\n81\t  url: string,\n82\t  {until = 'stream_end', token}: {until?: string; token?: string} = {}\n83\t): {client: WebSocket; finished: Promise<string[]>} {\n84\t  const client = connectClient(url, token);\n85\t  const finished = framesUntil(client, until);\n86\t  client.on('open', () => client.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'})));\n87\t  return {client, finished};\n88\t}\n89\t\n90\t/** The session id the client's `connected` frame announces, read after the fact; attach it before any frame arrives. */\n91\texport function announcedId(client: WebSocket): () => string {\n92\t  let sessionId = '';\n93\t  client.on('message', raw => {\n94\t    const frame = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as {type: string; sessionId?: string};\n95\t    if (frame.type === 'connected') sessionId = frame.sessionId ?? '';\n96\t  });\n97\t  return () => sessionId;\n98\t}\n99\t\n100\t/** Tears every socket and server down, so a red test reports instead of hanging the run. */\n101\texport async function stopEngines(): Promise<void> {\n102\t  for (const client of clients.splice(0)) client.terminate();\n103\t  for (const hal of servers.splice(0)) await hal.stop();\n104\t}\n105\t\n106\t/** The one session a store holds, for asserting what survived a close. */\n107\texport async function onlySession(store: SessionStore, sessionId: string): Promise<ChatSession | undefined> {\n108\t  return (await store.get(sessionId)) as ChatSession | undefined;\n109\t}\n110\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f8c91476-3213-4a4d-9278-f34d2b15649c","uuid":"51e87e34-5d28-4d9c-9337-edaf469df4bf","timestamp":"2026-11-02T23:56:36.421Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/97acaca6da89/workspace/target/src/transport/wsTestSupport.ts","content":"import type {AddressInfo} from 'node:net';\nimport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {Express} from 'express';\nimport {createServer} from './createServer.js';\nimport type {HalServer, HalServerOptions} from './createServer.js';\nimport {HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {MessageChunk} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\n\n// Shared by the suites that need a real upgrade over a real socket, which no double can stand in for.\n\nconst servers: Array<{stop: () => Promise<void>}> = [];\nconst clients: WebSocket[] = [];\n\n/** An orchestrator that answers once and stops, which is what commits the turn's assistant entry. */\nexport const answeringOrchestrator: ChatOrchestrator = {\n  processMessage: async () => 'hi',\n  async *processMessageStream(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n    yield {type: 'text', text: 'hi'};\n    yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n  },\n};\n\n/** Starts an engine on an ephemeral port and returns the URL a client should open. */\nexport async function startEngine(\n  sessionStore: SessionStore,\n  onDisconnect?: (sessionId: string) => void\n): Promise<string> {\n  const {url} = await startEngineWith({sessionStore, onDisconnect});\n  return url;\n}\n\n/** Starts an engine with whatever a suite overrides, returning the server itself beside the URL a client should open. */\nexport async function startEngineWith(\n  options: Partial<HalServerOptions> & Pick<HalServerOptions, 'sessionStore'>\n): Promise<{url: string; hal: HalServer}> {\n  const hal = createServer({\n    app: (() => undefined) as unknown as Express,\n    wsAuth: async () => ({id: 'u1'}),\n    orchestrator: answeringOrchestrator,\n    ...options,\n  });\n  await hal.start(0);\n  servers.push(hal);\n  const {port} = hal.server.address() as AddressInfo;\n  return {url: `ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/hal/ws/c1`, hal};\n}\n\n/** Opens a client that the suite's teardown will close, whether its test passed or failed. */\nexport function connectClient(url: string, token = 't'): WebSocket {\n  const client = new WebSocket(url, [HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL, token]);\n  clients.push(client);\n  return client;\n}\n\n/** A promise a test settles by hand, to hold a stream or a hook at a point of its choosing. */\nexport function deferred(): {until: Promise<void>; release: () => void} {\n  let release = (): void => undefined;\n  const until = new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    release = resolve;\n  });\n  return {until, release};\n}\n\n/** Resolves with every frame type seen up to and including the first `until` frame. */\nexport function framesUntil(client: WebSocket, until: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const seen: string[] = [];\n  return new Promise<string[]>(resolve => {\n    client.on('message', raw => {\n      const {type} = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as {type: string};\n      seen.push(type);\n      if (type === until) resolve(seen);\n    });\n  });\n}\n\n/** Opens a client that asks one question the moment the socket opens, resolving on the first `until` frame. */\nexport function askOnOpen(\n  url: string,\n  {until = 'stream_end', token}: {until?: string; token?: string} = {}\n): {client: WebSocket; finished: Promise<string[]>} {\n  const client = connectClient(url, token);\n  const finished = framesUntil(client, until);\n  client.on('open', () => client.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'})));\n  return {client, finished};\n}\n\n/** The session id the client's `connected` frame announces, read after the fact; attach it before any frame arrives. */\nexport function announcedId(client: WebSocket): () => string {\n  let sessionId = '';\n  client.on('message', raw => {\n    const frame = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as {type: string; sessionId?: string};\n    if (frame.type === 'connected') sessionId = frame.sessionId ?? '';\n  });\n  return () => sessionId;\n}\n\n/** Tears every socket and server down, so a red test reports instead of hanging the run. */\nexport async function stopEngines(): Promise<void> {\n  for (const client of clients.splice(0)) client.terminate();\n  for (const hal of servers.splice(0)) await hal.stop();\n}\n\n/** The one session a store holds, for asserting what survived a close. */\nexport async function onlySession(store: SessionStore, sessionId: string): Promise<ChatSession | undefined> {\n  return (await store.get(sessionId)) as ChatSession | undefined;\n}\n","numLines":110,"startLine":1,"totalLines":110}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_a8909ef5337c411582073ca7","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_9341001b628b49519e33ebf8","type":"tool_result","content":"import {once} from 'node:events';\nimport type {AddressInfo} from 'node:net';\nimport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {Express} from 'express';\nimport {createServer} from './createServer.js';\nimport {HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\nimport type {SessionResumeOptions} from './ws/sessionResume.js';\nimport {answeringOrchestrator} from './wsTestSupport.js';\nimport {InMemorySessionStore} from '../infrastructure/stores/inMemorySessionStore.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {MessageChunk} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ConnectedMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../types/session.js';\nimport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\nimport {setLogger} from '../shared/logger.js';\n\n// Real upgrades over a real socket: what a reconnect is sent is the contract, and no double can observe it.\n\ninterface Frame {\n  type: string;\n  sessionId?: string;\n  resumed?: boolean;\n  entryCount?: number;\n  resumeFailure?: string;\n  index?: number;\n  entry?: SessionEntry;\n}\n\nconst NEVER_ISSUED = '0f1e2d3c-4b5a-4968-8776-655443322110';\nconst RESUME_ON: SessionResumeOptions = {enabled: true};\nconst LATEST_ON: SessionResumeOptions = {enabled: true, latest: true};\nconst HALF_ANSWER: SessionEntry = {\n  role: 'assistant',\n  content: 'half',\n  timestamp: '2026-09-30T08:00:01.000Z',\n  isStreaming: true,\n};\n\n// A tool's suppression lands while the answer is still open, as when a provider ends a tool round with no stop.\nconst suppressingMidAnswer: ChatOrchestrator = {\n  processMessage: async () => 'before after',\n  async *processMessageStream(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n    yield {type: 'text', text: 'before'};\n    yield {type: 'suppress_output'} as unknown as MessageChunk;\n    yield {type: 'text', text: ' after'};\n    yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n  },\n};\n\nconst servers: Array<ReturnType<typeof createServer>> = [];\nconst clients: WebSocket[] = [];\n\nconst eventually = async (check: () => boolean): Promise<void> => {\n  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 50 && !check(); attempt++) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10));\n};\n\n// Silenced for the whole file: the server logs every connection and close, and these tests read frames, not logs.\nbeforeAll(() => {\n  setLogger({debug: () => undefined, info: () => undefined, warn: () => undefined, error: () => undefined});\n});\n\nafterAll(() => {\n  setLogger();\n});\n\n// Each server sees its sockets close before it stops, so no close is logged after the logger above is restored.\nafterEach(async () => {\n  for (const client of clients.splice(0)) client.terminate();\n  for (const hal of servers.splice(0)) {\n    const {wss} = hal;\n    await eventually(() => wss.clients.size === 0);\n    await hal.stop();\n  }\n});\n\n// The authenticated user is read per upgrade, so one test can reconnect as someone else.\nconst startEngine = async (\n  store: SessionStore,\n  resume?: SessionResumeOptions,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator = answeringOrchestrator\n) => {\n  const auth = {userId: 'u1'};\n  const connects: Array<{sessionId: string; resumed: boolean}> = [];\n  const disconnected: string[] = [];\n  const hal = createServer({\n    app: (() => undefined) as unknown as Express,\n    wsAuth: async () => ({id: auth.userId}),\n    sessionStore: store,\n    orchestrator,\n    resume,\n    onConnect: (session, {resumed}) => void connects.push({sessionId: session.sessionId, resumed}),\n    onDisconnect: sessionId => void disconnected.push(sessionId),\n  });\n  // Resolves once the server itself has seen its first socket close, which the client's own close can precede.\n  const firstServerClose = new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    hal.wss.once('connection', socket => socket.once('close', () => resolve()));\n  });\n  await hal.start(0);\n  servers.push(hal);\n  const {port} = hal.server.address() as AddressInfo;\n  return {auth, connects, disconnected, firstServerClose, base: `ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/hal/ws`};\n};\n\nconst open = (url: string, token = 't'): WebSocket => {\n  const client = new WebSocket(url, [HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL, token]);\n  clients.push(client);\n  return client;\n};\n\n// One question answered and the socket closed, returning the id the connected frame announced.\nconst firstTurn = async (base: string): Promise<string> => {\n  const client = open(base);\n  let sessionId = '';\n  await new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    client.on('message', raw => {\n      const frame = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as Frame;\n      if (frame.type === 'connected') sessionId = frame.sessionId ?? '';\n      if (frame.type === 'connected') client.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}));\n      if (frame.type === 'stream_end') resolve();\n    });","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f8c91476-3213-4a4d-9278-f34d2b15649c","uuid":"4e7f4304-fa64-4b11-b934-d66fee6adb51","timestamp":"2026-11-02T23:56:42.675Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"import {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_c922ab213dda4cf181683798","type":"tool_result","content":"import {jest} from '@jest/globals';\nimport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport {createMessageHandler} from './messageHandler.js';\nimport {createChatOrchestrator, TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport {ToolRegistry} from '../../orchestration/tools/registry.js';\nimport {PromptBuilder} from '../../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\nimport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk, ToolCall} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession, SessionEntry} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport {PerSessionLock} from '../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\nimport {deferred} from '../wsTestSupport.js';\n\n// The frames, in order, are the contract - above all for suppression, which retracts.\n\n// One line per frame; entries carry generated timestamps no assertion should read.\nconst wire = (sent: OutgoingMessage[]): string[] =>\n  sent.map(frame => {\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_upsert') {\n      const content = 'content' in frame.entry ? frame.entry.content : '';\n      return `upsert ${frame.index} ${frame.entry.role} ${JSON.stringify(content)}`;\n    }\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_delta') return `delta ${frame.index} ${JSON.stringify(frame.delta)}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_commit') return `commit ${frame.index}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_skip') return `skip ${frame.index}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'error') return `error ${frame.code}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'pong') return `pong ${frame.timestamp}`;\n    return frame.type;\n  });\n\nconst text = (value: string): MessageChunk => ({type: 'text', text: value});\nconst STOP: MessageChunk = {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\nconst SUPPRESS = {type: 'suppress_output'} as unknown as MessageChunk;\n\ninterface StreamScript {\n  failWith?: Error;\n  pauseAfter?: {chunks: number; until: Promise<void>};\n}\n\nconst harness = (chunks: MessageChunk[], {failWith, pauseAfter}: StreamScript = {}) => {\n  const sent: OutgoingMessage[] = [];\n  const ws = {send: (raw: string) => sent.push(JSON.parse(raw) as OutgoingMessage)} as unknown as WebSocket;\n\n  const stream = async function* (): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n    for (const [position, chunk] of chunks.entries()) {\n      if (pauseAfter && position === pauseAfter.chunks) await pauseAfter.until;\n      yield chunk;\n    }\n    if (failWith) throw failWith;\n  };\n\n  const processMessageStream = jest.fn(stream);\n  const orchestrator = {processMessageStream} as unknown as ChatOrchestrator;\n  const session: ChatSession = {sessionId: 's1', userId: 'u1', entries: []};\n\n  const handle = createMessageHandler(orchestrator);\n\n  return {\n    session,\n    processMessageStream,\n    sent,\n    frames: () => wire(sent),\n    send: (raw: unknown = {type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}, signal?: AbortSignal) =>\n      handle(ws, session, raw, signal),\n  };\n};\n\ndescribe('the websocket message handler', () => {\n  describe('dispatch', () => {\n    it('rejects an unparseable message and never starts a stream', async () => {\n      const h = harness([]);\n\n      await h.send({type: 'nonsense'});\n\n      const {calls} = h.processMessageStream.mock;\n\n      expect({frames: h.frames(), streams: calls.length}).toEqual({frames: ['error INVALID_MESSAGE'], streams: 0});\n    });\n\n    it('answers a ping with the timestamp it was given', async () => {\n      const h = harness([]);\n\n      await h.send({type: 'ping', timestamp: 1234});\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['pong 1234']);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('a plain assistant reply', () => {\n    it('sends the user entry, then the assistant entry, its delta, its commit and stream_end', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('hi there'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual([\n        'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n        'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n        'delta 1 \"hi there\"',\n        'commit 1',\n        'stream_end',\n      ]);\n    });\n\n    it('leaves the committed assistant entry in the session, no longer streaming', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('hi there'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const {entries} = h.session;\n\n      expect(entries[1]).toMatchObject({role: 'assistant', content: 'hi there', isStreaming: false});\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('thinking segments', () => {\n    it('commits the thinking entry before the assistant entry opens', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('<thinking>pondering</thinking>answer'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n----\nimport {validateMessage} from './validation.js';\n\n// One wire name for the client frame: the alias is gone, and the frame the validator returns is the exported shape.\n\ndescribe('the websocket frame validator', () => {\n  it('accepts user_message and returns the frame the exported union describes', () => {\n    const result = validateMessage({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'});\n\n    expect(result).toEqual({valid: true, data: {type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}});\n  });\n\n  it('rejects send_message, which is no longer a wire name', () => {\n    const result = validateMessage({type: 'send_message', content: 'hello'});\n\n    expect(result).toEqual({valid: false, error: 'Unknown message type: send_message'});\n  });\n\n  it('drops any field the frame does not define, rather than forwarding it', () => {\n    const result = validateMessage({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello', chatId: 'c1'});\n\n    expect(result).toEqual({valid: true, data: {type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}});\n  });\n});\n----\nimport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\n\nexport function sendJson(ws: WebSocket, message: OutgoingMessage): void {\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify(message));\n}\n\nexport function sendUpsert(ws: WebSocket, index: number, entry: SessionEntry): void {\n  sendJson(ws, {type: 'entry_upsert', index, entry});\n}\n\nexport function sendDelta(ws: WebSocket, index: number, delta: string): void {\n  sendJson(ws, {type: 'entry_delta', index, delta});\n}\n\nexport function sendCommit(ws: WebSocket, index: number): void {\n  sendJson(ws, {type: 'entry_commit', index});\n}\n\nexport function sendError(ws: WebSocket, code: string, message: string): void {\n  sendJson(ws, {type: 'error', code, message});\n}\n\nexport function sendSkip(ws: WebSocket, index: number): void {\n  sendJson(ws, {type: 'entry_skip', index});\n}\n\nexport function sendStreamEnd(ws: WebSocket): void {\n  sendJson(ws, {type: 'stream_end'});\n}","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f8c91476-3213-4a4d-9278-f34d2b15649c","uuid":"11eaa121-5f0e-4b30-9430-f7da9f310157","timestamp":"2026-11-02T23:56:50.099Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"import
